{"parentUuid":"05213d96-78cb-493b-98b7-dce0cbd6c75c","isSidechain":false,"promptId":"b8cfd43f-81e6-4053-8352-8512d3bf2add","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01BbnocUSBNmAFJWC6e9m9TX","type":"tool_result","content":"# Client Brief - AWM at Kenyon\n\n## Who runs the site?\n\nThe site is run by the officers of the organization, the 2 co-VPs maintain the site. All officers must log in and log out after every session. There will be passwords that are passed down from spring semester to fall semester each year.\n\n## What Positions have control over the site?\n\nCo-VPS are admin. Co-Presidents, Social Chair, and Treasurer are editors. Editors publish and delete posts, they will be able to edit pages but never delete them. The positions change so each new person will have different levels of computing expertise, so it needs to be easily accessible. The co-VPs are the only people able to add and change officer's roles.\n\n## Who is the site for?\n\nThe site is for members and curious students. Information on the site is about when and where Study Hall is and how to join. Study Hall is in Hayes 311, every Monday from 8pm to 10pm. We also have a link to a google form for snack requests.\n\n## Issue with the site right now\n\nThe old site means editing raw HTML. The old site is only HTML local files on a laptop. It was never put online, so people who aren't on the email list can't find anything yet. The time and place is in 3 files. The weekly email is currently our only form of communication.\n\n## What would make this better?\n\nA new officer can post the Monday Study Hall info without even learning HTML\n\n## Forms of communication\n\nThe weekly emails stay manual, with a link to the website post\n\n## Extra Notes\n\n- Meetings are called Study Halls.\n\n- An editor is never allowed to delete a page of the website.\n\n## Example of what we might send\n\nHello everybody!\n\nWe are CURRENTLY having our study hall tonight from 8-10 in Hayes 311. All are welcome and we will have lots of free snacks!\n\nCome and say hi!\n\n- AWM &lt;3\n\n----\n\"\"\"Content: Posts.\n\nOwns saving, status changes, Link generation, and deletion. Routes ask this\nmodule; they do not touch the database themselves. Any Editor or Admin may do\nanything to any Post, so there are no per-Post permission checks here.\n\nTimes are stored as UTC ISO strings and shown in US Eastern.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nfrom app.accounts import connect\n\nEASTERN = ZoneInfo(\"America/New_York\")\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    link text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references accounts(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    first_published_at text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n_SELECT = (\"select posts.*, accounts.display_name as author from posts\"\n           \" join accounts on accounts.id = posts.author_id\")\n\n\nclass EmptyTitle(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A Post must have a title.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef now() -> datetime:\n    \"\"\"The current time. Tests replace this to control dates.\"\"\"\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef _stamp() -> str:\n    return now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n\n\ndef init_db() -> None:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef eastern(stamp: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Show a stored UTC time as US Eastern, e.g. '2026-10-05 14:00'.\"\"\"\n    if not stamp:\n        return \"\"\n    when = datetime.strptime(stamp, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\").replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n    return when.astimezone(EASTERN).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n\n\ndef _slug(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_link(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = f\"{now().astimezone(EASTERN):%Y-%m-%d}-{_slug(title)}\"\n    link, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"select 1 from posts where link = ?\", (link,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        link = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return link\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise EmptyTitle(\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef list_posts() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} order by posts.created_at desc, posts.id desc\"\n                            ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef get_post(post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} where posts.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef create_post(title: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Save a new Draft. Its Link is made now and never changes.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    stamp = _stamp()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"insert into posts (title, link, body, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_link(conn, title), body, author_id, stamp, stamp))\n        return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(post_id: int, title: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Change the text of a Post. The Author and the Link stay as they were.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"update posts set title = ?, body = ?, updated_at = ?\"\n                           \" where id = ?\", (title, body, _stamp(), post_id))\n        return cur.rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef set_status(post_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Mark a Post 'published' or 'draft'. First-published time is set once.\"\"\"\n    assert status in (\"draft\", \"published\")\n    stamp = _stamp()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" first_published_at = case when ? = 'published' then\"\n            \" coalesce(first_published_at, ?) else first_published_at end\"\n            \" where id = ?\", (status, stamp, status, stamp, post_id))\n        return cur.rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete_post(post_id: int) -> bool:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id = ?\", (post_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef seed_demo_posts() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create a Published and a Draft demo Post. Safe to run twice.\"\"\"\n    init_db()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        if conn.execute(\"select 1 from posts\").fetchone():\n            return\n        author = conn.execute(\"select id from accounts where display_name = 'Social Chair'\"\n                              ).fetchone()\n        if author is None:\n            return\n    published = create_post(\n        \"Study Hall tonight\",\n        \"Study Hall is **tonight**, 8-10pm in Hayes 311. Bring something to work on.\",\n        author[\"id\"])\n    set_status(published, \"published\")\n    create_post(\"Snack request form is open\",\n                \"Draft: tell us what snacks you want. Add the form link here.\",\n                author[\"id\"])\n----\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py:82:    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"nobody\"}, tmp_path)\ntests/conftest.py:4:in as a seeded Account of that role, with CSRF protection in place:\ntests/conftest.py:9:Every test runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the demo\ntests/conftest.py:22:# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\ntests/conftest.py:24:DEMO_USERS = {\ntests/conftest.py:40:    \"\"\"Give each test its own database, seeded with the demo Accounts.\"\"\"\ntests/conftest.py:51:    accounts.seed_demo_accounts(DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\ntests/conftest.py:52: }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nThe yearly passing of Account passwords from the spring semester's It is the only kind of dated content.\n_Avoid_: announcement, update\n\n**Study Hall reminder**:\nThe weekly Monday Post about that night's Study Hall. Nearly every Post is one.\n\n**Author**:\nThe Position whose Account wrote a Post. Officers see it; Visitors do not.\n\n**Weekly email**:\nThe message Officers send by hand each week. Its text is the same as that week's Study Hall reminder, plus a link to the Post.\n\n**Page**:\nA permanent part of the site's structure. There are three: Home, About, and Join & Snacks.\n\n**Home**:\nThe Page that holds the standing Study Hall time and place and shows the latest Published Post beneath it. We also have a link to a google form for snack requests.\n\n## Issue with the site right now\n\nThe old site means editing raw HTML. The old site is only HTML local files on a laptop. It was never put online, so people who aren't on the email list can't find anything yet. The time and place is in 3 files. The weekly email is currently our only form of communication.\n\n## What would make this better?\n\nA new officer can post the Monday Study Hall info without even learning HTML\n\n## Forms of communication\n\nThe weekly emails stay manual, with a link to the website post\n\n## Extra Notes\n\n- Meetings are called Study Halls.\n\n- An editor is never allowed to delete a page of the website.\n\n## Example of what we might send\n\nHello everybody!\n\nWe are CURRENTLY having our study hall tonight from 8-10 in Hayes 311. All are welcome and we will have lots of free snacks!\n\nCome and say hi!\n\n- AWM &lt;3\n\n----\n\"\"\"Content: Posts.\n\nOwns saving, status changes, Link generation, and deletion. Routes ask this\nmodule; they do not touch the database themselves. Any Editor or Admin may do\nanything to any Post, so there are no per-Post permission checks here.\n\nTimes are stored as UTC ISO strings and shown in US Eastern.\n\"\"\"\nfrom __future__ import annotations\n\nimport re\nimport sqlite3\nfrom datetime import datetime, timezone\nfrom zoneinfo import ZoneInfo\n\nfrom app.accounts import connect\n\nEASTERN = ZoneInfo(\"America/New_York\")\n\nSCHEMA = \"\"\"\ncreate table if not exists posts (\n    id integer primary key,\n    title text not null,\n    link text not null unique,\n    body text not null default '',\n    status text not null default 'draft' check (status in ('draft', 'published')),\n    author_id integer not null references accounts(id),\n    created_at text not null,\n    updated_at text not null,\n    first_published_at text\n)\n\"\"\"\n\n_SELECT = (\"select posts.*, accounts.display_name as author from posts\"\n           \" join accounts on accounts.id = posts.author_id\")\n\n\nclass EmptyTitle(ValueError):\n    \"\"\"A Post must have a title.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef now() -> datetime:\n    \"\"\"The current time. Tests replace this to control dates.\"\"\"\n    return datetime.now(timezone.utc)\n\n\ndef _stamp() -> str:\n    return now().strftime(\"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\")\n\n\ndef init_db() -> None:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        conn.execute(SCHEMA)\n\n\ndef eastern(stamp: str | None) -> str:\n    \"\"\"Show a stored UTC time as US Eastern, e.g. '2026-10-05 14:00'.\"\"\"\n    if not stamp:\n        return \"\"\n    when = datetime.strptime(stamp, \"%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ\").replace(tzinfo=timezone.utc)\n    return when.astimezone(EASTERN).strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M\")\n\n\ndef _slug(title: str) -> str:\n    return re.sub(r\"[^a-z0-9]+\", \"-\", title.lower()).strip(\"-\") or \"post\"\n\n\ndef _unique_link(conn: sqlite3.Connection, title: str) -> str:\n    base = f\"{now().astimezone(EASTERN):%Y-%m-%d}-{_slug(title)}\"\n    link, n = base, 1\n    while conn.execute(\"select 1 from posts where link = ?\", (link,)).fetchone():\n        n += 1\n        link = f\"{base}-{n}\"\n    return link\n\n\ndef _clean_title(title: str) -> str:\n    title = title.strip()\n    if not title:\n        raise EmptyTitle(\"A Post needs a title.\")\n    return title\n\n\ndef list_posts() -> list[sqlite3.Row]:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} order by posts.created_at desc, posts.id desc\"\n                            ).fetchall()\n\n\ndef get_post(post_id: int) -> sqlite3.Row | None:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(f\"{_SELECT} where posts.id = ?\", (post_id,)).fetchone()\n\n\ndef create_post(title: str, body: str, author_id: int) -> int:\n    \"\"\"Save a new Draft. Its Link is made now and never changes.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    stamp = _stamp()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"insert into posts (title, link, body, author_id, created_at, updated_at)\"\n            \" values (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)\",\n            (title, _unique_link(conn, title), body, author_id, stamp, stamp))\n        return cur.lastrowid\n\n\ndef update_post(post_id: int, title: str, body: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Change the text of a Post. The Author and the Link stay as they were.\"\"\"\n    title = _clean_title(title)\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\"update posts set title = ?, body = ?, updated_at = ?\"\n                           \" where id = ?\", (title, body, _stamp(), post_id))\n        return cur.rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef set_status(post_id: int, status: str) -> bool:\n    \"\"\"Mark a Post 'published' or 'draft'. First-published time is set once.\"\"\"\n    assert status in (\"draft\", \"published\")\n    stamp = _stamp()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        cur = conn.execute(\n            \"update posts set status = ?, updated_at = ?,\"\n            \" first_published_at = case when ? = 'published' then\"\n            \" coalesce(first_published_at, ?) else first_published_at end\"\n            \" where id = ?\", (status, stamp, status, stamp, post_id))\n        return cur.rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef delete_post(post_id: int) -> bool:\n    with connect() as conn:\n        return conn.execute(\"delete from posts where id = ?\", (post_id,)).rowcount == 1\n\n\ndef seed_demo_posts() -> None:\n    \"\"\"Create a Published and a Draft demo Post. Safe to run twice.\"\"\"\n    init_db()\n    with connect() as conn:\n        if conn.execute(\"select 1 from posts\").fetchone():\n            return\n        author = conn.execute(\"select id from accounts where display_name = 'Social Chair'\"\n                              ).fetchone()\n        if author is None:\n            return\n    published = create_post(\n        \"Study Hall tonight\",\n        \"Study Hall is **tonight**, 8-10pm in Hayes 311. Bring something to work on.\",\n        author[\"id\"])\n    set_status(published, \"published\")\n    create_post(\"Snack request form is open\",\n                \"Draft: tell us what snacks you want. Add the form link here.\",\n                author[\"id\"])\n----\ntests/test_ctx_guard.py:82:    out = run(\"ctx_guard.py\", {\"session_id\": \"nobody\"}, tmp_path)\ntests/conftest.py:4:in as a seeded Account of that role, with CSRF protection in place:\ntests/conftest.py:9:Every test runs against its own temporary database, seeded with the demo\ntests/conftest.py:22:# Matches scripts/seed_demo.py. Passwords come from the environment there; in\ntests/conftest.py:24:DEMO_USERS = {\ntests/conftest.py:40:    \"\"\"Give each test its own database, seeded with the demo Accounts.\"\"\"\ntests/conftest.py:51:    accounts.seed_demo_accounts(DEMO_USERS[\"admin\"][\"password\"],\ntests/conftest.py:52:   assert run_seed(db).returncode == 0\n---\n{% extends \"base.html\" %}\n{% block content %}\n  <h1>{{ title }}</h1>\n  {% if items %}\n    <ul>{% for item in items %}<li><a href=\"{{ item.href }}\">{{ item.title }}</a></li>{% endfor %}</ul>\n  {% else %}\n    <p>Nothing published yet.</p>\n  {% endif %}\n{% endblock %}\nThe yearly passing of Account passwords from the spring semester's Officers to the fall semester's.\n\n**Visitor**:\nA member or curious student reading the public site. A Visitor never logs in.\n\n### Content\n\n**Study Hall**:\nAWM's weekly meeting.\n_Avoid_: meeting\n\n**Post**:\nA dated news item on the site. It is the only kind of dated content.\n_Avoid_: announcement, update\n\n**Study Hall reminder**:\nThe weekly Monday Post about that night's Study Hall. Nearly every Post is one.\n\n**Author**:\nThe Position whose Account wrote a Post. Officers see it; Visitors do not.\n\n**Weekly email**:\nThe message Officers send by hand each week. Its text is the same as that week's Study Hall reminder, plus a link to the Post.\n\n**Page**:\nA permanent part of the site's structure. There are three: Home, About, and Join & Snacks.\n\n**Home**:\nThe Page that holds the standing Study Hall time and place and shows the latest Published Post beneath it. Home always exists and is always Published.\n\n**Past posts**:\nThe list of older Published Posts that Visitors reach from Home.
